File: api-gateway/pom.xml
================
 1: <?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
 2: <project xmlns="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0"
 3:          xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
 4:          xsi:schemaLocation="http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0 http://maven.apache.org/xsd/maven-4.0.0.xsd">
 5:     <parent>
 6:         <artifactId>demo</artifactId>
 7:         <groupId>club.newtech.qbike</groupId>
 8:         <version>0.0.1-SNAPSHOT</version>
 9:     </parent>
10:     <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>
11:     <artifactId>api-gateway</artifactId>
12:     <packaging>jar</packaging>
13:     <properties>
14:         <project.build.sourceEncoding>UTF-8</project.build.sourceEncoding>
15:         <project.reporting.outputEncoding>UTF-8</project.reporting.outputEncoding>
16:         <java.version>1.8</java.version>
17:         <springfox.version>2.7.0</springfox.version>
18:     </properties>
19:     <dependencies>
20:         <dependency>
21:             <groupId>org.springframework.cloud</groupId>
22:             <artifactId>spring-cloud-starter-netflix-eureka-client</artifactId>
23:         </dependency>
24:         <dependency>
25:             <groupId>org.springframework.cloud</groupId>
26:             <artifactId>spring-cloud-starter-zipkin</artifactId>
27:         </dependency>
28:         <dependency>
29:             <groupId>org.springframework.amqp</groupId>
30:             <artifactId>spring-rabbit</artifactId>
31:         </dependency>
32:         <dependency>
33:             <groupId>org.springframework.cloud</groupId>
34:             <artifactId>spring-cloud-starter-netflix-zuul</artifactId>
35:         </dependency>
36:     </dependencies>
37:     <build>
38:         <plugins>
39:             <plugin>
40:                 <groupId>com.spotify</groupId>
41:                 <artifactId>docker-maven-plugin</artifactId>
42:                 <executions>
43:                     <execution>
44:                         <id>build-image</id>
45:                         <phase>package</phase>
46:                         <goals>
47:                             <goal>build</goal>
48:                         </goals>
49:                     </execution>
50:                 </executions>
51:                 <configuration>
52:                     <imageName>${docker.image.prefix}/${project.artifactId}</imageName>
53:                     <baseImage>java</baseImage>
54:                     <entryPoint>["java", "-jar", "/${project.build.finalName}.jar", "--spring.profiles.active=docker"]]</entryPoint>
55:                     <resources>
56:                         <resource>
57:                             <targetPath>/</targetPath>
58:                             <directory>${project.build.directory}</directory>
59:                             <include>${project.build.finalName}.jar</include>
60:                         </resource>
61:                     </resources>
62:                 </configuration>
63:             </plugin>
64:         </plugins>
65:     </build>
66: </project>

================
File: intention/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/intention/controller/RibbonHystrixController.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.intention.controller;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.controller.bean.MyIntention;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo.Customer;
 4: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo.DriverStatusVo;
 5: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.service.IntentionService;
 6: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.infrastructure.PositionApi;
 7: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.infrastructure.UserRibbonHystrixApi;
 8: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 9: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
10: import java.util.Collection;
11: @RestController
12: public class RibbonHystrixController {
13:     @Autowired
14:     private UserRibbonHystrixApi userRibbonHystrixApi;
15:     @Autowired
16:     private IntentionService intentionService;
17:     @Autowired
18:     private PositionApi positionApi;
19:     @GetMapping("/ribbon/{id}")
20:     public Customer findById(@PathVariable Integer id) {
21:         return this.userRibbonHystrixApi.findById(id);
22:     }
23:     @GetMapping("/ribbon/match")
24:     public Collection<DriverStatusVo> match(double longitude, double latitude) {
25:         return this.positionApi.match(longitude, latitude);
26:     }
27:     @PostMapping("/intentions/place")
28:     public void place(@RequestBody
29:                               MyIntention myIntention) {
30:         intentionService.placeIntention(myIntention.getUserId(),
31:                 myIntention.getStartLongitude(), myIntention.getStartLatitude(),
32:                 myIntention.getDestLongitude(), myIntention.getDestLatitude());
33:     }
34:     @PostMapping("/intention/confirm")
35:     public boolean confirm(int driverId, int intentionId) {
36:         return intentionService.confirmIntention(driverId, intentionId);
37:     }
38: }

14: @Data
15: @ToString
16: @Accessors(fluent = false, chain = true)
17: @Entity
18: @Table(name = "t_intention")
19: public class Intention {
20:     @Id
21:     @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
22:     private int mid;
23:     private Double startLongitude;
24:     private Double startLatitude;
25:     private Double destLongitude;
26:     private Double destLatitude;
27:     @Embedded
28:     private Customer customer;
29:     @Enumerated(value = STRING)
30:     @Column(length = 32, nullable = false)
31:     private Status status;
32:     @Embedded
33:     private DriverVo selectedDriver;
34:     @Temporal(TemporalType.TIMESTAMP)
35:     private Date updated = new Date();
36:     @OneToMany(mappedBy = "intention", fetch = FetchType.EAGER, cascade = CascadeType.ALL)
37:     private List<Candidate> candidates = new ArrayList<>();
38:     public boolean canMatchDriver() {
39:         if (status.equals(Status.Inited)) {
40:             return true;
41:         } else {
42:             return false;
43:         }
44:     }
45:     private boolean canConfirm() {
46:         if (status.equals(Status.UnConfirmed)) {
47:             return true;
48:         } else {
49:             return false;
50:         }
51:     }
52:     public boolean waitingConfirm() {
53:         if (canMatchDriver()) {
54:             this.status = Status.UnConfirmed;
55:             this.updated = new Date();
56:             return true;
57:         } else {
58:             return false;
59:         }
60:     }
61:     public boolean fail() {
62:         if (this.status == Status.Inited) {
63:             this.status = Status.Failed;
64:             this.updated = new Date();
65:             return true;
66:         } else {
67:             return false;
68:         }
69:     }
70:     public int confirmIntention(DriverVo driverVo) {
71:         if (!canConfirm()) {
72:             return -1;
73:         }
74:         if (candidates.stream().map(Candidate::getDriverId).noneMatch(id -> id == driverVo.getId())) {
75:             return -2;
76:         }
77:         if (this.selectedDriver == null) {
78:             this.selectedDriver = driverVo;
79:             this.status = Status.Confirmed;
80:             this.updated = new Date();
81:             return 0;
82:         } else {
83:             return -3;
84:         }
85:     }
86: }

File: intention/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/intention/domain/core/vo/IntentionTask.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo;
 2: import lombok.Getter;
 3: import lombok.ToString;
 4: import java.util.concurrent.Delayed;
 5: import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
 6: @Getter
 7: @ToString
 8: public class IntentionTask implements Delayed {
 9:     private final long NANO_ORIGIN = System.nanoTime();
10:     private final long executionTime;
11:     private int intenionId;
12:     private int repeatTimes;
13:     public IntentionTask(int intenionId, long time, TimeUnit unit, int repeatTimes) {
14:         this.intenionId = intenionId;
15:         this.repeatTimes = repeatTimes;
16:         this.executionTime = TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.convert(time, unit);
17:     }
18:     final long now() {
19:         return System.nanoTime() - NANO_ORIGIN;
20:     }
21:     @Override
22:     public long getDelay(TimeUnit unit) {
23:         long d = unit.convert(executionTime - now(), TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS);
24:         return d;
25:     }
26:     @Override
27:     public int compareTo(Delayed other) {
28:         if (other == this)
29:             return 0;
30:         if (other instanceof IntentionTask) {
31:             IntentionTask x = (IntentionTask) other;
32:             long diff = executionTime - x.executionTime;
33:             if (diff < 0)
34:                 return -1;
35:             else if (diff > 0)
36:                 return 1;
37:         }
38:         long d = (getDelay(TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS) - other.getDelay(TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS));
39:         return (d == 0) ? 0 : ((d < 0) ? -1 : 1);
40:     }
41: }

 1: package club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo;
 2: import lombok.Data;
 3: import lombok.ToString;
 4: import java.util.Date;
 5: @Data
 6: @ToString
 7: public class Lock implements Comparable<Lock> {
 8:     private final String name;
 9:     private final String value;
10:     private final Date expires;
11:     public boolean isExpired() {
12:         return expires.before(new Date());
13:     }
14:     @Override
15:     public int compareTo(Lock other) {
16:         return expires.compareTo(other.expires);
17:     }
18: }

File: intention/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/intention/domain/service/IntentionService.java
================
  1: package club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.service;
  2: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.controller.bean.IntentionVo;
  3: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.root.Intention;
  4: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo.*;
  5: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.exception.LockException;
  6: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.repository.CandidateRepository;
  7: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.repository.IntentionRepository;
  8: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.infrastructure.PositionApi;
  9: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.infrastructure.UserRibbonHystrixApi;
 10: import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;
 11: import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
 12: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 13: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 14: import org.springframework.amqp.rabbit.core.RabbitTemplate;
 15: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 16: import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
 17: import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Async;
 18: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 19: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
 20: import java.util.Collection;
 21: import java.util.Date;
 22: import java.util.List;
 23: import java.util.concurrent.DelayQueue;
 24: import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
 25: import static java.util.stream.Collectors.toList;
 26: @Service
 27: public class IntentionService {
 28:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(IntentionService.class);
 29:     @Autowired
 30:     IntentionRepository intentionRepository;
 31:     @Autowired
 32:     UserRibbonHystrixApi userApi;
 33:     @Autowired
 34:     RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
 35:     @Autowired
 36:     PositionApi positionApi;
 37:     @Autowired
 38:     CandidateRepository candidateRepository;
 39:     @Autowired
 40:     LockService lockService;
 41:     @Autowired
 42:     ObjectMapper objectMapper;
 43:     @Autowired
 44:     RabbitTemplate rabbitTemplate;
 45:     private DelayQueue<IntentionTask> intentions = new DelayQueue<>();
 46:     private static Candidate fromDriverStatus(DriverStatusVo driverStatusVo, Intention intention) {
 47:         Candidate candidate = new Candidate();
 48:         candidate.setDriverId(driverStatusVo.getDId());
 49:         candidate.setCreated(new Date());
 50:         candidate.setDriverMobile(driverStatusVo.getDriver().getMobile());
 51:         candidate.setDriverName(driverStatusVo.getDriver().getUserName());
 52:         candidate.setLongitude(driverStatusVo.getCurrentLongitude());
 53:         candidate.setLatitude(driverStatusVo.getCurrentLatitude());
 54:         candidate.setIntention(intention);
 55:         return candidate;
 56:     }
 57:     @Transactional
 58:     public void placeIntention(int userId, Double startLongitude, Double startLatitude,
 59:                                Double destLongitude, Double destLatitude) {
 60:         Customer customer = userApi.findById(userId);
 61:         Intention intention = new Intention()
 62:                 .setStartLongitude(startLongitude)
 63:                 .setStartLatitude(startLatitude)
 64:                 .setDestLongitude(destLongitude)
 65:                 .setDestLatitude(destLatitude)
 66:                 .setCustomer(customer)
 67:                 .setStatus(Status.Inited);
 68:         intentionRepository.save(intention);
 69:         IntentionTask task = new IntentionTask(intention.getMid(), 2L, TimeUnit.SECONDS, 0);
 70:         intentions.put(task);
 71:     }
 72:     @Transactional
 73:     public void sendNotification(Collection<DriverStatusVo> result, Intention intention) {
 74:         result.stream()
 75:                 .map(vo -> fromDriverStatus(vo, intention))
 76:                 .forEach(candidate -> candidateRepository.save(candidate));
 77:         intention.waitingConfirm();
 78:         intentionRepository.save(intention);
 79:     }
 80:     @Transactional
 81:     public boolean confirmIntention(int driverId, int intentionId) {
 82:         String lockName = "intention" + intentionId;
 83:         Lock lock = null;
 84:         try {
 85:             lock = lockService.create(lockName);
 86:             Intention intention = intentionRepository.findById(intentionId).orElse(null);
 87:             DriverVo driverVo = userApi.findDriverById(driverId);
 88:             int ret = intention.confirmIntention(driverVo);
 89:             LOGGER.info("{}司机抢单{}结果为{}", driverId, intentionId, ret);
 90:             if (ret == 0) {
 91:                 intentionRepository.save(intention);
 92:                 IntentionVo intentionVo = new IntentionVo().setIntentionId(intention.getMid())
 93:                         .setCustomerId(intention.getCustomer().getCustomerId())
 94:                         .setDestLat(intention.getDestLatitude())
 95:                         .setDestLong(intention.getDestLongitude())
 96:                         .setStartLong(intention.getStartLongitude())
 97:                         .setStartLat(intention.getStartLatitude())
 98:                         .setDriverId(intention.getSelectedDriver().getId());
 99:                 try {
100:                     rabbitTemplate.convertAndSend("intention", objectMapper.writeValueAsString(intentionVo));
101:                 } catch (JsonProcessingException e) {
102:                     LOGGER.error("convert message fail" + intentionVo, e);
103:                 }
104:                 return true;
105:             } else {
106:                 return false;
107:             }
108:         } catch (LockException e) {
109:             LOGGER.error("try lock error ", e);
110:             return false;
111:         } finally {
112:             if (lock != null) {
113:                 lockService.release(lockName, lock.getValue());
114:             }
115:         }
116:     }
117:     @Transactional
118:     public void matchFail(Intention intention) {
119:         intention.fail();
120:         intentionRepository.save(intention);
121:     }
122:     @Async
123:     public void handleTask() {
124:         LOGGER.info("start handling intention task loop");
125:         for (; ; ) {
126:             try {
127:                 IntentionTask task = intentions.take();
128:                 if (task != null) {
129:                     LOGGER.info("got a task {}", task.getIntenionId());
130:                     Intention intention = intentionRepository.findById(task.getIntenionId()).orElse(null);
131:                     if (intention.canMatchDriver()) {
132:                         Collection<DriverStatusVo> result = positionApi.match(intention.getStartLongitude(), intention.getStartLatitude());
133:                         if (result.size() > 0) {
134:                             List<String> names = result.stream().map(s -> s.getDriver().getUserName()).collect(toList());
135:                             LOGGER.info("匹配司机{}，将向他们发送抢单请求", names);
136:                             sendNotification(result, intention);
137:                         } else {
138:                             LOGGER.info("没有匹配到司机，放入队列继续等待");
139:                             retryMatch(task, intention);
140:                         }
141:                     } else {
142:                     }
143:                 }
144:             } catch (Exception e) {
145:                 LOGGER.error("error happened", e);
146:             }
147:         }
148:     }
149:     private boolean retryMatch(IntentionTask task, Intention intention) {
150:         int times = task.getRepeatTimes() + 1;
151:         LOGGER.info("task 已经被循环{} 次", times);
152:         if (times > 5) {
153:             matchFail(intention);
154:             return true;
155:         }
156:         IntentionTask newTask = new IntentionTask(intention.getMid(), 2 * times, TimeUnit.SECONDS, times);
157:         this.intentions.put(newTask);
158:         return false;
159:     }
160: }

File: intention/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/intention/domain/service/LockService.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.service;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo.Lock;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.exception.LockExistsException;
 4: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.exception.LockNotHeldException;
 5: import java.util.Set;
 6: public interface LockService {
 7:     Iterable<Lock> findAll();
 8:     Lock create(String name) throws LockExistsException;
 9:     Set<Lock> createMultiLock(Set<String> names) throws LockExistsException;
10:     boolean release(String name, String value) throws LockNotHeldException;
11:     Lock refresh(String name, String value) throws LockNotHeldException;
12: }

================
File: intention/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/intention/domain/service/RedisLockService.java
================
  1: package club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.service;
  2: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo.Lock;
  3: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.exception.LockExistsException;
  4: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.exception.LockNotHeldException;
  5: import lombok.Setter;
  6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
  7: import org.springframework.dao.DataAccessException;
  8: import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisOperations;
  9: import org.springframework.data.redis.core.SessionCallback;
 10: import org.springframework.data.redis.core.StringRedisTemplate;
 11: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 12: import java.util.*;
 13: import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
 14: import static java.util.stream.Collectors.toMap;
 15: @Service
 16: public class RedisLockService implements LockService {
 17:     private static final String DEFAULT_LOCK_PREFIX = "qbike.lock.";
 18:     @Autowired
 19:     StringRedisTemplate redisOperations;
 20:     private String prefix = DEFAULT_LOCK_PREFIX;
 21:     @Setter
 22:     private long expiry = 30000;
 23:     public void setPrefix(String prefix) {
 24:         if (!prefix.endsWith(".")) {
 25:             prefix = prefix + ".";
 26:         }
 27:         this.prefix = prefix;
 28:     }
 29:     @Override
 30:     public Iterable<Lock> findAll() {
 31:         Set<String> keys = redisOperations.keys(prefix + "*");
 32:         Set<Lock> locks = new LinkedHashSet<Lock>();
 33:         for (String key : keys) {
 34:             Date expires = new Date(System.currentTimeMillis() + redisOperations.getExpire(key, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS));
 35:             locks.add(new Lock(nameForKey(key), redisOperations.opsForValue().get(key), expires));
 36:         }
 37:         return locks;
 38:     }
 39:     @Override
 40:     public Lock create(String name) {
 41:         String stored = getValue(name);
 42:         if (stored != null) {
 43:             throw new LockExistsException();
 44:         }
 45:         String value = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
 46:         String key = keyForName(name);
 47:         if (!redisOperations.opsForValue().setIfAbsent(key, value)) {
 48:             throw new LockExistsException();
 49:         }
 50:         redisOperations.expire(key, expiry, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 51:         Date expires = new Date(System.currentTimeMillis() + expiry);
 52:         return new Lock(name, value, expires);
 53:     }
 54:     @Override
 55:     public Set<Lock> createMultiLock(Set<String> names) {
 56:         Set<Lock> locks = redisOperations.execute(new SessionCallback<Set<Lock>>() {
 57:             @Override
 58:             public Set<Lock> execute(RedisOperations operations) throws DataAccessException {
 59:                 Set<Lock> locks = new HashSet<>();
 60:                 Map<String, String> mset =
 61:                         names.stream()
 62:                                 .collect(toMap(name -> keyForName(name), key -> UUID.randomUUID().toString()));
 63:                 boolean b = operations.opsForValue()
 64:                         .multiSetIfAbsent(mset);
 65:                 if (b) {
 66:                     operations.multi();
 67:                     for (String name : names) {
 68:                         String key = keyForName(name);
 69:                         operations.expire(key, expiry, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
 70:                         Date expires = new Date(System.currentTimeMillis() + expiry);
 71:                         Lock lock = new Lock(name, mset.get(key), expires);
 72:                         locks.add(lock);
 73:                     }
 74:                     operations.exec();
 75:                     return locks;
 76:                 } else {
 77:                     throw new LockExistsException();
 78:                 }
 79:             }
 80:         });
 81:         return locks;
 82:     }
 83:     @Override
 84:     public boolean release(String name, String value) {
 85:         String stored = getValue(name);
 86:         if (stored != null && value.equals(stored)) {
 87:             String key = keyForName(name);
 88:             redisOperations.delete(key);
 89:             return true;
 90:         }
 91:         if (stored != null) {
 92:             throw new LockNotHeldException();
 93:         }
 94:         return false;
 95:     }
 96:     @Override
 97:     public Lock refresh(String name, String value) {
 98:         String key = keyForName(name);
 99:         String stored = getValue(name);
100:         if (stored != null && value.equals(stored)) {
101:             Date expires = new Date(System.currentTimeMillis() + expiry);
102:             redisOperations.expire(key, expiry, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
103:             return new Lock(name, value, expires);
104:         }
105:         throw new LockNotHeldException();
106:     }
107:     private String getValue(String name) {
108:         String key = keyForName(name);
109:         String stored = redisOperations.opsForValue().get(key);
110:         return stored;
111:     }
112:     private String nameForKey(String key) {
113:         if (!key.startsWith(prefix)) {
114:             throw new IllegalStateException("Key (" + key + ") does not start with prefix (" + prefix + ")");
115:         }
116:         return key.substring(prefix.length());
117:     }
118:     private String keyForName(String name) {
119:         return prefix + name;
120:     }
121: }

================
File: intention/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/intention/infrastructure/PositionApi.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.intention.infrastructure;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo.DriverStatusVo;
 3: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixCommand;
 4: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 5: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.core.ParameterizedTypeReference;
 8: import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
 9: import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
10: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
11: import org.springframework.web.client.RestTemplate;
12: import java.util.ArrayList;
13: import java.util.Collection;
14: @Service
15: public class PositionApi {
16:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(PositionApi.class);
17:     @Autowired
18:     private RestTemplate restTemplate;
19:     @HystrixCommand(fallbackMethod = "defaultMatch")
20:     public Collection<DriverStatusVo> match(double longitude, double latitude) {
21:         ResponseEntity<Collection<DriverStatusVo>> matchReponse =
22:                 restTemplate.exchange(
23:                         String.format("http://qbike-trip/trips/match?longitude=%s&latitude=%s", longitude, latitude),
24:                         HttpMethod.GET, null,
25:                         new ParameterizedTypeReference<Collection<DriverStatusVo>>() {
26:                         }
27:                 );
28:         return matchReponse.getBody();
29:     }
30:     public Collection<DriverStatusVo> defaultMatch(double longitude, double latitude) {
31:         return new ArrayList<>();
32:     }
33: }

================
File: intention/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/intention/infrastructure/UserRibbonHystrixApi.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.intention.infrastructure;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo.Customer;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.intention.domain.core.vo.DriverVo;
 4: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixCommand;
 5: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 6: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 9: import org.springframework.web.client.RestTemplate;
10: import java.util.Map;
11: @Service
12: public class UserRibbonHystrixApi {
13:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(UserRibbonHystrixApi.class);
14:     @Autowired
15:     private RestTemplate restTemplate;
16:     @HystrixCommand
17:     public Customer findById(Integer id) {
18:         Map ret = restTemplate.getForObject("http://QBIKE-UC/users/" + id, Map.class);
19:         Customer customerVo = new Customer();
20:         customerVo.setCustomerId(id);
21:         customerVo.setCustomerMobile(String.valueOf(ret.get("mobile")));
22:         customerVo.setCustomerName(String.valueOf(ret.get("userName")));
23:         customerVo.setUserType(String.valueOf(ret.get("type")));
24:         return customerVo;
25:     }
26:     @HystrixCommand
27:     public DriverVo findDriverById(Integer id) {
28:         Map ret = restTemplate.getForObject("http://QBIKE-UC/users/" + id, Map.class);
29:         DriverVo driverVo = new DriverVo();
30:         driverVo.setId(id);
31:         driverVo.setMobile(String.valueOf(ret.get("mobile")));
32:         driverVo.setUserName(String.valueOf(ret.get("userName")));
33:         driverVo.setType(String.valueOf(ret.get("type")));
34:         return driverVo;
35:     }
36:     public Customer fallback(Integer id) {
37:         UserRibbonHystrixApi.LOGGER.info("异常发生，进入fallback方法，接收的参数：customerId = {}", id);
38:         Customer customer = new Customer();
39:         customer.setCustomerId(-1);
40:         customer.setCustomerName("default username");
41:         customer.setCustomerMobile("0000");
42:         return customer;
43:     }
44: }

File: order/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/order/controller/OrderController.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.controller;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.Events;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.StateRequest;
 4: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.exception.OrderRuntimeException;
 5: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.repository.OrderRepository;
 6: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.service.FsmService;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
 9: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
10: import java.util.Arrays;
11: import java.util.List;
12: import java.util.UUID;
13: @RestController
14: public class OrderController {
15:     @Autowired
16:     FsmService fsmService;
17:     @Autowired
18:     OrderRepository orderRepository;
19:     @PostMapping("/order/cancel")
20:     public List<String> cancelOrder(int driverId, String orderId) {
21:         StateRequest stateRequest = new StateRequest();
22:         stateRequest.setEvent(Events.CANCEL);
23:         stateRequest.setData(orderRepository.findById(orderId).get());
24:         stateRequest.setUId(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
25:         stateRequest.setOrderId(orderId);
26:         stateRequest.setUserId(driverId);
27:         try {
28:             fsmService.changeState(stateRequest);
29:             return Arrays.asList("success", "");
30:         } catch (OrderRuntimeException oe) {
31:             return Arrays.asList(oe.getErrorCode(), oe.getErrorMessage());
32:         }
33:     }
34:     @PostMapping("/order/aboard")
35:     public List<String> aboard(int driverId, String orderId) {
36:         StateRequest stateRequest = new StateRequest();
37:         stateRequest.setEvent(Events.ABOARD);
38:         stateRequest.setData(orderRepository.findById(orderId).get());
39:         stateRequest.setUId(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
40:         stateRequest.setOrderId(orderId);
41:         stateRequest.setUserId(driverId);
42:         try {
43:             fsmService.changeState(stateRequest);
44:             return Arrays.asList("success", "");
45:         } catch (OrderRuntimeException oe) {
46:             return Arrays.asList(oe.getErrorCode(), oe.getErrorMessage());
47:         }
48:     }
49: }

File: order/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/order/domain/core/vo/FlowState.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo;
 2: import java.util.HashMap;
 3: import java.util.Map;
 4: public enum FlowState {
 5:     WAITING_ABOARD("WAITING_ABOARD", "等待上车"),
 6:     WAITING_ARRIVE("WAITING_ARRIVE", "等待到达目的地"),
 7:     UNPAY("UNPAY", "等待支付"),
 8:     PAYING("PAYING", "已支付待确认"),
 9:     WAITING_COMMENT("WAITING_COMMENT", "等待评论"),
10:     CLOSED("CLOSED", "订单关闭"),
11:     CANCELED("CANCELED", "订单取消");
12:     private static Map<String, FlowState> flowStateMap = new HashMap<>();
13:     static {
14:         flowStateMap.put(WAITING_ABOARD.getStateId(), WAITING_ABOARD);
15:         flowStateMap.put(WAITING_ARRIVE.getStateId(), WAITING_ARRIVE);
16:         flowStateMap.put(UNPAY.getStateId(), UNPAY);
17:         flowStateMap.put(PAYING.getStateId(), PAYING);
18:         flowStateMap.put(WAITING_COMMENT.getStateId(), WAITING_COMMENT);
19:         flowStateMap.put(CLOSED.getStateId(), CLOSED);
20:         flowStateMap.put(CANCELED.getStateId(), CANCELED);
21:     }
22:     private String stateId;
23:     private String description;
24:     FlowState(String stateId, String description) {
25:         this.stateId = stateId;
26:         this.description = description;
27:     }
28:     public static FlowState forValue(String stateId) {
29:         return flowStateMap.get(stateId);
30:     }
31:     public String toValue() {
32:         return this.getStateId();
33:     }
34:     public String getStateId() {
35:         return stateId;
36:     }
37:     public String getDescription() {
38:         return description;
39:     }
40: }

File: order/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/order/domain/exception/OrderRuntimeException.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.exception;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.order.util.SpringContextHolder;
 3: import lombok.Data;
 4: import lombok.EqualsAndHashCode;
 5: import org.apache.commons.lang3.StringUtils;
 6: import org.springframework.context.MessageSource;
 7: import org.springframework.context.NoSuchMessageException;
 8: import java.util.Locale;
 9: @Data
10: @EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
11: public class OrderRuntimeException extends RuntimeException {
12:     private String errorCode = "";
13:     private String errorMessage = "";
14:     private Object[] params;
15:     private MessageSource messageSource = SpringContextHolder.getBean(MessageSource.class);
16:     public OrderRuntimeException() {
17:         super();
18:         this.errorCode = "000000";
19:     }
20:     public OrderRuntimeException(String errorCode) {
21:         super();
22:         this.errorCode = errorCode;
23:         this.params = null;
24:     }
25:     public OrderRuntimeException(String errorCode, Object[] params) {
26:         super();
27:         this.errorCode = errorCode;
28:         this.params = params;
29:     }
30:     public OrderRuntimeException(String errorCode, Throwable cause) {
31:         super(cause);
32:         this.errorCode = errorCode;
33:         this.params = null;
34:     }
35:     public OrderRuntimeException(String errorCode, Object[] params, Throwable cause) {
36:         super(cause);
37:         this.errorCode = errorCode;
38:         this.params = params;
39:     }
40:     public OrderRuntimeException(Throwable cause) {
41:         super(cause);
42:     }
43:     public String getErrorMessage() {
44:         String msg = "";
45:         Throwable cause = this.getCause();
46:         String errorCode = this.getErrorCode();
47:         if (StringUtils.isNotEmpty(errorCode) && StringUtils.isEmpty(msg)) {
48:             //2、如果有异常码，以异常码对应的提示信息为准
49:             msg = this.getMessage(errorCode, this.getParams());
50:         }
51:         if (StringUtils.isEmpty(msg) && cause != null) {
52:             msg = cause.getMessage();
53:         }
54:         if (StringUtils.isEmpty(msg)) {
55:             //3、异常码为空 & msg为空，提示系统异常
56:             msg = this.getMessage("000000", this.getParams());
57:         }
58:         return msg;
59:     }
60:     private String getMessage(String code, Object[] params) {
61:         try {
62:             return messageSource.getMessage(code, params, Locale.CHINA);
63:         } catch (NoSuchMessageException e) {
64:             return code;
65:         }
66:     }
67: }

File: order/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/order/domain/service/FsmService.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.service;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.root.Order;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.Events;
 4: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.FlowState;
 5: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.StateRequest;
 6: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.exception.OrderRuntimeException;
 7: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.repository.OrderRepository;
 8: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 9: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
10: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
11: import org.springframework.statemachine.StateMachine;
12: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
13: @Service
14: public class FsmService {
15:     public static final String UUID_KEY = "UUID_KEY";
16:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(FsmService.class);
17:     @Autowired
18:     private OrderRepository orderRepository;
19:     @Autowired
20:     private StateMachineBuilderFactory builderFactory;
21:     public void changeState(StateRequest request) {
22:         Order order = orderRepository.findById(request.getOrderId()).orElse(null);
23:         if (order == null) {
24:             throw new OrderRuntimeException("030001", new Object[]{request.getOrderId()});
25:         }
26:         StateMachine<FlowState, Events> stateMachine = builderFactory.create(order);
27:         if (request.getData() != null) {
28:             stateMachine.getExtendedState().getVariables().put(StateRequest.class, request.getData());
29:         }
30:         stateMachine.getExtendedState().getVariables().put(UUID_KEY, request.getUId());
31:         boolean isSend = stateMachine.sendEvent(request.getEvent());
32:         if (!isSend) {
33:             LOGGER.error(String.format("无法从状态[%s]转向 => [%s]", FlowState.forValue(order.getOrderStatus()), request.getEvent()));
34:             throw new OrderRuntimeException("010003");
35:         }
36:         Exception error = stateMachine.getExtendedState().get(Exception.class, Exception.class);
37:         if (error != null) {
38:             if (error.getClass().equals(OrderRuntimeException.class)) {
39:                 throw (OrderRuntimeException) error;
40:             } else {
41:                 throw new OrderRuntimeException("000000", error);
42:             }
43:         }
44:     }
45: }

File: order/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/order/domain/service/OrderService.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.service;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.root.Order;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.CustomerVo;
 4: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.DriverVo;
 5: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.FlowState;
 6: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.IntentionVo;
 7: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.exception.OrderRuntimeException;
 8: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.repository.OrderRepository;
 9: import club.newtech.qbike.order.infrastructure.UserRibbonHystrixApi;
10: import club.newtech.qbike.order.util.SequenceFactory;
11: import org.slf4j.Logger;
12: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
13: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
14: import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
15: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
16: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
17: import java.util.Date;
18: @Service
19: public class OrderService {
20:     public static final String UUID_KEY = "UUID_KEY";
21:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrderService.class);
22:     @Autowired
23:     UserRibbonHystrixApi userService;
24:     @Autowired
25:     private RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
26:     @Autowired
27:     private OrderRepository orderRepository;
28:     @Autowired
29:     private SequenceFactory sequenceFactory;
30:     private String generateOrderId() {
31:         return "T" + String.format("%010d", sequenceFactory.generate("order"));
32:     }
33:     @Transactional
34:     public Order createOrder(IntentionVo intention) {
35:         Order order = new Order();
36:         order.setOid(generateOrderId());
37:         CustomerVo customerVo = userService.findCustomerById(intention.getCustomerId());
38:         DriverVo driverVo = userService.findDriverById(intention.getDriverId());
39:         order.setCustomer(customerVo);
40:         order.setDriver(driverVo);
41:         order.setOrderStatus(FlowState.WAITING_ABOARD.toValue());
42:         order.setOpened(new Date());
43:         order.setStartLong(intention.getStartLong());
44:         order.setStartLat(intention.getStartLat());
45:         order.setDestLong(intention.getDestLong());
46:         order.setDestLat(intention.getDestLat());
47:         order.setIntentionId(String.valueOf(intention.getIntentionId()));
48:         orderRepository.save(order);
49:         return order;
50:     }
51:     @Transactional
52:     public void aboard(Order order) {
53:         order.setOrderStatus(FlowState.WAITING_ARRIVE.toValue());
54:         order.setOpened(new Date());
55:         orderRepository.save(order);
56:     }
57:     @Transactional
58:     public void cancel(Order order) {
59:         Date currentTime = new Date();
60:         if ((currentTime.getTime() - order.getOpened().getTime()) <= 3 * 60 * 1000L) {
61:             order.setOrderStatus(FlowState.CANCELED.toValue());
62:             orderRepository.save(order);
63:         } else {
64:             throw new OrderRuntimeException("040001");
65:         }
66:     }
67: }

================
File: order/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/order/domain/service/OrderStateMachineBuilder.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.service;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.root.Order;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.Events;
 4: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.FlowState;
 5: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.StateRequest;
 6: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 7: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 8: import org.springframework.beans.factory.BeanFactory;
 9: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
10: import org.springframework.statemachine.StateMachine;
11: import org.springframework.statemachine.action.Action;
12: import org.springframework.statemachine.config.StateMachineBuilder;
13: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
14: import java.util.EnumSet;
15: @Component
16: public class OrderStateMachineBuilder {
17:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrderStateMachineBuilder.class);
18:     @Autowired
19:     OrderService orderService;
20:     @Autowired
21:     ErrorAction errorAction;
22:     public StateMachine<FlowState, Events> build(FlowState initState, BeanFactory beanFactory) throws Exception {
23:         StateMachineBuilder.Builder<FlowState, Events> builder = StateMachineBuilder.builder();
24:         builder.configureConfiguration()
25:                 .withConfiguration()
26:                 .machineId("orderFSM")
27:                 .beanFactory(beanFactory);
28:         builder.configureStates()
29:                 .withStates()
30:                 .initial(initState)
31:                 .end(FlowState.CLOSED)
32:                 .end(FlowState.CANCELED)
33:                 .states(EnumSet.allOf(FlowState.class));
34:         builder.configureTransitions()
35:                 .withExternal()
36:                 .source(FlowState.WAITING_ABOARD).target(FlowState.WAITING_ARRIVE)
37:                 .event(Events.ABOARD)
38:                 .action(aboard(), errorAction)
39:                 .and()
40:                 .withExternal()
41:                 .source(FlowState.WAITING_ABOARD).target(FlowState.CANCELED)
42:                 .event(Events.CANCEL)
43:                 .action(cancel(), errorAction)
44:                 .and()
45:                 .withExternal()
46:                 .source(FlowState.WAITING_ARRIVE).target(FlowState.UNPAY)
47:                 .event(Events.ARRIVE)
48:                 .and()
49:                 .withExternal()
50:                 .source(FlowState.UNPAY).target(FlowState.PAYING)
51:                 .event(Events.PAY)
52:                 .and()
53:                 .withExternal()
54:                 .source(FlowState.PAYING).target(FlowState.WAITING_COMMENT)
55:                 .event(Events.PAY_CALLBACK)
56:                 .and()
57:                 .withExternal()
58:                 .source(FlowState.WAITING_COMMENT).target(FlowState.CLOSED)
59:                 .event(Events.COMMENT);
60:         return builder.build();
61:     }
62:     public Action<FlowState, Events> aboard() {
63:         return stateContext -> {
64:             Order order = stateContext.getExtendedState().get(StateRequest.class, Order.class);
65:             String uuId = stateContext.getExtendedState().get(OrderService.UUID_KEY, String.class);
66:             order.setOrderStatus(FlowState.WAITING_ARRIVE.toValue());
67:             orderService.aboard(order);
68:         };
69:     }
70:     public Action<FlowState, Events> cancel() {
71:         return stateContext -> {
72:             Order order = stateContext.getExtendedState().get(StateRequest.class, Order.class);
73:             String uuId = stateContext.getExtendedState().get(OrderService.UUID_KEY, String.class);
74:             order.setOrderStatus(FlowState.CANCELED.toValue());
75:             orderService.cancel(order);
76:         };
77:     }
78: }

File: order/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/order/domain/service/StateMachineBuilderFactory.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.service;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.root.Order;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.Events;
 4: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.FlowState;
 5: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.exception.OrderRuntimeException;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.BeanFactory;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import org.springframework.statemachine.StateMachine;
 9: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
10: @Component
11: public class StateMachineBuilderFactory {
12:     @Autowired
13:     private OrderStateMachineBuilder orderStateMachineBuilder;
14:     @Autowired
15:     private BeanFactory beanFactory;
16:     public StateMachine<FlowState, Events> create(Order order) {
17:         FlowState flowState = FlowState.forValue(order.getOrderStatus());
18:         try {
19:             StateMachine<FlowState, Events> sm = orderStateMachineBuilder.build(flowState, beanFactory);
20:             sm.start();
21:             sm.getExtendedState().getVariables().put(Order.class, order);
22:             return sm;
23:         } catch (Exception e) {
24:             throw new OrderRuntimeException(String.format("创建状态[%s]失败 => [%s]", flowState, e.getCause()));
25:         }
26:     }
27: }

 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.infrastructure;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.CustomerVo;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.order.domain.core.vo.DriverVo;
 4: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixCommand;
 5: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 6: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 8: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 9: import org.springframework.web.client.RestTemplate;
10: import java.util.Map;
11: @Service
12: public class UserRibbonHystrixApi {
13:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(UserRibbonHystrixApi.class);
14:     @Autowired
15:     private RestTemplate restTemplate;
16:     @HystrixCommand()
17:     public CustomerVo findCustomerById(Integer id) {
18:         Map ret = restTemplate.getForObject("http://QBIKE-UC/users/" + id, Map.class);
19:         CustomerVo customerVo = new CustomerVo();
20:         customerVo.setCustomerId(id);
21:         customerVo.setCustomerMobile(String.valueOf(ret.get("mobile")));
22:         customerVo.setCustomerName(String.valueOf(ret.get("userName")));
23:         return customerVo;
24:     }
25:     @HystrixCommand()
26:     public DriverVo findDriverById(Integer id) {
27:         Map ret = restTemplate.getForObject("http://QBIKE-UC/users/" + id, Map.class);
28:         DriverVo driverVo = new DriverVo();
29:         driverVo.setDriverId(id);
30:         driverVo.setDriverMobile(String.valueOf(ret.get("mobile")));
31:         driverVo.setDriverName(String.valueOf(ret.get("userName")));
32:         return driverVo;
33:     }
34:     public CustomerVo fallback(Integer id) {
35:         LOGGER.info("异常发生，进入fallback方法，接收的参数：customerId = {}", id);
36:         CustomerVo customer = new CustomerVo();
37:         customer.setCustomerId(-1);
38:         customer.setCustomerName("default username");
39:         customer.setCustomerMobile("0000");
40:         return customer;
41:     }
42:     public DriverVo fallbackDriver(Integer id) {
43:         LOGGER.info("异常发生，进入fallback方法，接收的参数：customerId = {}", id);
44:         DriverVo driverVo = new DriverVo();
45:         driverVo.setDriverId(-1);
46:         driverVo.setDriverName("default");
47:         driverVo.setDriverMobile("0000");
48:         return driverVo;
49:     }
50: }

File: order/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/order/util/SequenceFactory.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.util;
 2: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 3: import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
 4: import org.springframework.data.redis.support.atomic.RedisAtomicLong;
 5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 6: import java.util.Date;
 7: import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
 8: @Service
 9: public class SequenceFactory {
10:     @Autowired
11:     RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
12:     public void set(String key, int value, Date expireTime) {
13:         RedisAtomicLong counter = new RedisAtomicLong(key, redisTemplate.getConnectionFactory());
14:         counter.set(value);
15:         counter.expireAt(expireTime);
16:     }
17:     public void set(String key, int value, long timeout, TimeUnit unit) {
18:         RedisAtomicLong counter = new RedisAtomicLong(key, redisTemplate.getConnectionFactory());
19:         counter.set(value);
20:         counter.expire(timeout, unit);
21:     }
22:     public long generate(String key) {
23:         RedisAtomicLong counter = new RedisAtomicLong(key, redisTemplate.getConnectionFactory());
24:         return counter.incrementAndGet();
25:     }
26:     public long generate(String key, Date expireTime) {
27:         RedisAtomicLong counter = new RedisAtomicLong(key, redisTemplate.getConnectionFactory());
28:         counter.expireAt(expireTime);
29:         return counter.incrementAndGet();
30:     }
31:     public long generate(String key, int increment) {
32:         RedisAtomicLong counter = new RedisAtomicLong(key, redisTemplate.getConnectionFactory());
33:         return counter.addAndGet(increment);
34:     }
35:     public long generate(String key, int increment, Date expireTime) {
36:         RedisAtomicLong counter = new RedisAtomicLong(key, redisTemplate.getConnectionFactory());
37:         counter.expireAt(expireTime);
38:         return counter.addAndGet(increment);
39:     }
40: }

 1: package club.newtech.qbike.order.util;
 2: import org.springframework.beans.BeansException;
 3: import org.springframework.context.ApplicationContext;
 4: import org.springframework.context.ApplicationContextAware;
 5: import org.springframework.context.annotation.Lazy;
 6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
 7: @Component
 8: @Lazy(false)
 9: public class SpringContextHolder implements ApplicationContextAware {
10:     private static ApplicationContext applicationContext;
11:     @Override
12:     public void setApplicationContext(ApplicationContext applicationContext) throws BeansException {
13:         setApplicationContextWithStatic(applicationContext);
14:     }
15:     private static void  setApplicationContextWithStatic(ApplicationContext applicationContext){
16:         SpringContextHolder.applicationContext = applicationContext;
17:     }
18:     public static ApplicationContext getApplicationContext() {
19:         return applicationContext;
20:     }
21:     @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
22:     public static <T> T getBean(String name) throws BeansException {
23:         return (T)applicationContext.getBean(name);
24:     }
25:     public static <T> T getBean(Class<T> type) throws BeansException {
26:         return applicationContext.getBean(type);
27:     }
28: }

 1: package club.newtech.qbike.trip.controller;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.core.root.DriverStatus;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.service.PositionService;
 4: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 5: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
 8: import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
 9: import java.util.Collection;
10: @RestController
11: public class PositionController {
12:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(PositionController.class);
13:     @Autowired
14:     PositionService positionService;
15:     @GetMapping("/trips/updatePosition")
16:     public void positionUpdate(Integer driverId, Double longitude, Double latitude) {
17:         LOGGER.info(String.format("update position %s %s %s", driverId, longitude, latitude));
18:         positionService.updatePosition(driverId, longitude, latitude);
19:     }
20:     @GetMapping("/trips/match")
21:     public Collection<DriverStatus> match(Double longitude, Double latitude) {
22:         return positionService.matchDriver(longitude, latitude);
23:     }
24: }

================
File: position/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/trip/domain/service/PositionService.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.service;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.core.Status;
 3: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.core.root.DriverStatus;
 4: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.core.vo.Driver;
 5: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.core.vo.Position;
 6: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.repository.DriverStatusRepo;
 7: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.repository.PositionRepository;
 8: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.infrastructure.UserRibbonHystrixApi;
 9: import org.slf4j.Logger;
10: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
11: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
12: import org.springframework.data.geo.Circle;
13: import org.springframework.data.geo.Distance;
14: import org.springframework.data.geo.GeoResults;
15: import org.springframework.data.geo.Point;
16: import org.springframework.data.redis.connection.RedisGeoCommands;
17: import org.springframework.data.redis.core.RedisTemplate;
18: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
19: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
20: import java.util.ArrayList;
21: import java.util.Collection;
22: import java.util.Date;
23: import java.util.List;
24: import static java.util.stream.Collectors.toList;
25: @Service
26: @Transactional
27: public class PositionService {
28:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(PositionService.class);
29:     @Autowired
30:     DriverStatusRepo driverStatusRepo;
31:     @Autowired
32:     UserRibbonHystrixApi userService;
33:     @Autowired
34:     RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
35:     @Autowired
36:     PositionRepository positionRepository;
37:     public void updatePosition(Integer driverId, Double longitude, Double latitude) {
38:         Date current = new Date();
39:         Position position = new Position();
40:         position.setDriverId(String.valueOf(driverId));
41:         position.setPositionLongitude(longitude);
42:         position.setPositionLatitude(latitude);
43:         position.setStatus(Status.ONLINE);
44:         position.setUploadTime(current);
45:         positionRepository.save(position);
46:         DriverStatus driverStatus = driverStatusRepo.findByDriver_Id(driverId);
47:         if (driverStatus != null) {
48:             driverStatus.setCurrentLongitude(longitude);
49:             driverStatus.setCurrentLatitude(latitude);
50:             driverStatus.setUpdateTime(current);
51:             driverStatusRepo.save(driverStatus);
52:         } else {
53:             Driver driver = userService.findById(driverId);
54:             driverStatus = new DriverStatus();
55:             driverStatus.setDriver(driver);
56:             driverStatus.setCurrentLongitude(longitude);
57:             driverStatus.setCurrentLatitude(latitude);
58:             driverStatus.setUpdateTime(current);
59:             driverStatus.setStatus(Status.ONLINE);
60:             driverStatus.setDId(driverId);
61:             driverStatusRepo.save(driverStatus);
62:         }
63:         redisTemplate.opsForGeo().geoAdd("Drivers", new Point(longitude, latitude), String.valueOf(driverId));
64:         LOGGER.info("position update " + driverStatus);
65:     }
66:     public Collection<DriverStatus> matchDriver(double longitude, double latitude) {
67:         Circle circle = new Circle(new Point(longitude, latitude),
68:                 new Distance(500, RedisGeoCommands.DistanceUnit.METERS));
69:         GeoResults<RedisGeoCommands.GeoLocation<String>> result =
70:                 redisTemplate.opsForGeo().geoRadius("Drivers", circle);
71:         if (result.getContent().size() == 0) {
72:             LOGGER.info("没找到匹配司机");
73:             return new ArrayList<>();
74:         } else {
75:             List<String> drivers = result.getContent()
76:                     .stream()
77:                     .map(x -> x.getContent().getName())
78:                     .collect(toList());
79:             LOGGER.info("获取附近司机为{}", drivers);
80:             return drivers.stream().map(Integer::parseInt)
81:                     .map(id -> driverStatusRepo.findByDriver_Id(id)).collect(toList());
82:         }
83:     }
84: }

================
File: position/src/main/java/club/newtech/qbike/trip/infrastructure/UserRibbonHystrixApi.java
================
 1: package club.newtech.qbike.trip.infrastructure;
 2: import club.newtech.qbike.trip.domain.core.vo.Driver;
 3: import com.netflix.hystrix.contrib.javanica.annotation.HystrixCommand;
 4: import org.slf4j.Logger;
 5: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
 6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
 7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
 8: import org.springframework.web.client.RestTemplate;
 9: @Service
10: public class UserRibbonHystrixApi {
11:     private static final Logger LOGGER = LoggerFactory.getLogger(UserRibbonHystrixApi.class);
12:     @Autowired
13:     private RestTemplate restTemplate;
14:     @HystrixCommand
15:     public Driver findById(Integer id) {
16:         Driver driver = this.restTemplate.getForObject("http://QBIKE-UC/users/" + id, Driver.class);
17:         driver.setId(id);
18:         return driver;
19:     }
20:     public Driver fallback(Integer id) {
21:         UserRibbonHystrixApi.LOGGER.info("异常发生，进入fallback方法，接收的参数：customerId = {}", id);
22:         Driver driver = new Driver();
23:         driver.setId(-1);
24:         driver.setUserName("default driver");
25:         driver.setMobile("0000");
26:         return driver;
27:     }
28: }

46: eureka:
47:   client:
48:     serviceUrl:
49:       defaultZone: http://${EUREKA_HOST}:8761/eureka/

================
File: uc/mvnw
================
  1: #!/bin/sh
  2: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  3: # Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  4: # or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  5: # distributed with this work for additional information
  6: # regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  7: # to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  8: # "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  9: # with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
 10: #
 11: #    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 12: #
 13: # Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 14: # software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 15: # "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 16: # KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 17: # specific language governing permissions and limitations
 18: # under the License.
 19: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 20: 
 21: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 22: # Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 23: #
 24: # Required ENV vars:
 25: # ------------------
 26: #   JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 27: #
 28: # Optional ENV vars
 29: # -----------------
 30: #   M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 31: #   MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 32: #     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 33: #       set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 34: #   MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 35: # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 36: 
 37: if [ -z "$MAVEN_SKIP_RC" ] ; then
 38: 
 39:   if [ -f /etc/mavenrc ] ; then
 40:     . /etc/mavenrc
 41:   fi
 42: 
 43:   if [ -f "$HOME/.mavenrc" ] ; then
 44:     . "$HOME/.mavenrc"
 45:   fi
 46: 
 47: fi
 48: 
 49: # OS specific support.  $var _must_ be set to either true or false.
 50: cygwin=false;
 51: darwin=false;
 52: mingw=false
 53: case "`uname`" in
 54:   CYGWIN*) cygwin=true ;;
 55:   MINGW*) mingw=true;;
 56:   Darwin*) darwin=true
 57:     # Use /usr/libexec/java_home if available, otherwise fall back to /Library/Java/Home
 58:     # See https://developer.apple.com/library/mac/qa/qa1170/_index.html
 59:     if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
 60:       if [ -x "/usr/libexec/java_home" ]; then
 61:         export JAVA_HOME="`/usr/libexec/java_home`"
 62:       else
 63:         export JAVA_HOME="/Library/Java/Home"
 64:       fi
 65:     fi
 66:     ;;
 67: esac
 68: 
 69: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
 70:   if [ -r /etc/gentoo-release ] ; then
 71:     JAVA_HOME=`java-config --jre-home`
 72:   fi
 73: fi
 74: 
 75: if [ -z "$M2_HOME" ] ; then
 76:   ## resolve links - $0 may be a link to maven's home
 77:   PRG="$0"
 78: 
 79:   # need this for relative symlinks
 80:   while [ -h "$PRG" ] ; do
 81:     ls=`ls -ld "$PRG"`
 82:     link=`expr "$ls" : '.*-> \(.*\)$'`
 83:     if expr "$link" : '/.*' > /dev/null; then
 84:       PRG="$link"
 85:     else
 86:       PRG="`dirname "$PRG"`/$link"
 87:     fi
 88:   done
 89: 
 90:   saveddir=`pwd`
 91: 
 92:   M2_HOME=`dirname "$PRG"`/..
 93: 
 94:   # make it fully qualified
 95:   M2_HOME=`cd "$M2_HOME" && pwd`
 96: 
 97:   cd "$saveddir"
 98:   # echo Using m2 at $M2_HOME
 99: fi
100: 
101: # For Cygwin, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
102: if $cygwin ; then
103:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
104:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$M2_HOME"`
105:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
106:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --unix "$JAVA_HOME"`
107:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
108:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --unix "$CLASSPATH"`
109: fi
110: 
111: # For Migwn, ensure paths are in UNIX format before anything is touched
112: if $mingw ; then
113:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
114:     M2_HOME="`(cd "$M2_HOME"; pwd)`"
115:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
116:     JAVA_HOME="`(cd "$JAVA_HOME"; pwd)`"
117:   # TODO classpath?
118: fi
119: 
120: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ]; then
121:   javaExecutable="`which javac`"
122:   if [ -n "$javaExecutable" ] && ! [ "`expr \"$javaExecutable\" : '\([^ ]*\)'`" = "no" ]; then
123:     # readlink(1) is not available as standard on Solaris 10.
124:     readLink=`which readlink`
125:     if [ ! `expr "$readLink" : '\([^ ]*\)'` = "no" ]; then
126:       if $darwin ; then
127:         javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
128:         javaExecutable="`cd \"$javaHome\" && pwd -P`/javac"
129:       else
130:         javaExecutable="`readlink -f \"$javaExecutable\"`"
131:       fi
132:       javaHome="`dirname \"$javaExecutable\"`"
133:       javaHome=`expr "$javaHome" : '\(.*\)/bin'`
134:       JAVA_HOME="$javaHome"
135:       export JAVA_HOME
136:     fi
137:   fi
138: fi
139: 
140: if [ -z "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
141:   if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME"  ] ; then
142:     if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
143:       # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
144:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java"
145:     else
146:       JAVACMD="$JAVA_HOME/bin/java"
147:     fi
148:   else
149:     JAVACMD="`which java`"
150:   fi
151: fi
152: 
153: if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
154:   echo "Error: JAVA_HOME is not defined correctly." >&2
155:   echo "  We cannot execute $JAVACMD" >&2
156:   exit 1
157: fi
158: 
159: if [ -z "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
160:   echo "Warning: JAVA_HOME environment variable is not set."
161: fi
162: 
163: CLASSWORLDS_LAUNCHER=org.codehaus.plexus.classworlds.launcher.Launcher
164: 
165: # traverses directory structure from process work directory to filesystem root
166: # first directory with .mvn subdirectory is considered project base directory
167: find_maven_basedir() {
168: 
169:   if [ -z "$1" ]
170:   then
171:     echo "Path not specified to find_maven_basedir"
172:     return 1
173:   fi
174: 
175:   basedir="$1"
176:   wdir="$1"
177:   while [ "$wdir" != '/' ] ; do
178:     if [ -d "$wdir"/.mvn ] ; then
179:       basedir=$wdir
180:       break
181:     fi
182:     # workaround for JBEAP-8937 (on Solaris 10/Sparc)
183:     if [ -d "${wdir}" ]; then
184:       wdir=`cd "$wdir/.."; pwd`
185:     fi
186:     # end of workaround
187:   done
188:   echo "${basedir}"
189: }
190: 
191: # concatenates all lines of a file
192: concat_lines() {
193:   if [ -f "$1" ]; then
194:     echo "$(tr -s '\n' ' ' < "$1")"
195:   fi
196: }
197: 
198: BASE_DIR=`find_maven_basedir "$(pwd)"`
199: if [ -z "$BASE_DIR" ]; then
200:   exit 1;
201: fi
202: 
203: export MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=${MAVEN_BASEDIR:-"$BASE_DIR"}
204: echo $MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR
205: MAVEN_OPTS="$(concat_lines "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/jvm.config") $MAVEN_OPTS"
206: 
207: # For Cygwin, switch paths to Windows format before running java
208: if $cygwin; then
209:   [ -n "$M2_HOME" ] &&
210:     M2_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$M2_HOME"`
211:   [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] &&
212:     JAVA_HOME=`cygpath --path --windows "$JAVA_HOME"`
213:   [ -n "$CLASSPATH" ] &&
214:     CLASSPATH=`cygpath --path --windows "$CLASSPATH"`
215:   [ -n "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR" ] &&
216:     MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=`cygpath --path --windows "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR"`
217: fi
218: 
219: WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
220: 
221: exec "$JAVACMD" \
222:   $MAVEN_OPTS \
223:   -classpath "$MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR/.mvn/wrapper/maven-wrapper.jar" \
224:   "-Dmaven.home=${M2_HOME}" "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=${MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR}" \
225:   ${WRAPPER_LAUNCHER} $MAVEN_CONFIG "$@"

================
File: uc/mvnw.cmd
================
  1: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  2: @REM Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one
  3: @REM or more contributor license agreements.  See the NOTICE file
  4: @REM distributed with this work for additional information
  5: @REM regarding copyright ownership.  The ASF licenses this file
  6: @REM to you under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the
  7: @REM "License"); you may not use this file except in compliance
  8: @REM with the License.  You may obtain a copy of the License at
  9: @REM
 10: @REM    http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
 11: @REM
 12: @REM Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing,
 13: @REM software distributed under the License is distributed on an
 14: @REM "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY
 15: @REM KIND, either express or implied.  See the License for the
 16: @REM specific language governing permissions and limitations
 17: @REM under the License.
 18: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 19: 
 20: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 21: @REM Maven2 Start Up Batch script
 22: @REM
 23: @REM Required ENV vars:
 24: @REM JAVA_HOME - location of a JDK home dir
 25: @REM
 26: @REM Optional ENV vars
 27: @REM M2_HOME - location of maven2's installed home dir
 28: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO - set to 'on' to enable the echoing of the batch commands
 29: @REM MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE - set to 'on' to wait for a key stroke before ending
 30: @REM MAVEN_OPTS - parameters passed to the Java VM when running Maven
 31: @REM     e.g. to debug Maven itself, use
 32: @REM set MAVEN_OPTS=-Xdebug -Xrunjdwp:transport=dt_socket,server=y,suspend=y,address=8000
 33: @REM MAVEN_SKIP_RC - flag to disable loading of mavenrc files
 34: @REM ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 35: 
 36: @REM Begin all REM lines with '@' in case MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO is 'on'
 37: @echo off
 38: @REM enable echoing my setting MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO to 'on'
 39: @if "%MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%" == "on"  echo %MAVEN_BATCH_ECHO%
 40: 
 41: @REM set %HOME% to equivalent of $HOME
 42: if "%HOME%" == "" (set "HOME=%HOMEDRIVE%%HOMEPATH%")
 43: 
 44: @REM Execute a user defined script before this one
 45: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPre
 46: @REM check for pre script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
 47: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.bat"
 48: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_pre.cmd"
 49: :skipRcPre
 50: 
 51: @setlocal
 52: 
 53: set ERROR_CODE=0
 54: 
 55: @REM To isolate internal variables from possible post scripts, we use another setlocal
 56: @setlocal
 57: 
 58: @REM ==== START VALIDATION ====
 59: if not "%JAVA_HOME%" == "" goto OkJHome
 60: 
 61: echo.
 62: echo Error: JAVA_HOME not found in your environment. >&2
 63: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 64: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 65: echo.
 66: goto error
 67: 
 68: :OkJHome
 69: if exist "%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe" goto init
 70: 
 71: echo.
 72: echo Error: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory. >&2
 73: echo JAVA_HOME = "%JAVA_HOME%" >&2
 74: echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the >&2
 75: echo location of your Java installation. >&2
 76: echo.
 77: goto error
 78: 
 79: @REM ==== END VALIDATION ====
 80: 
 81: :init
 82: 
 83: @REM Find the project base dir, i.e. the directory that contains the folder ".mvn".
 84: @REM Fallback to current working directory if not found.
 85: 
 86: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%MAVEN_BASEDIR%
 87: IF NOT "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%"=="" goto endDetectBaseDir
 88: 
 89: set EXEC_DIR=%CD%
 90: set WDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
 91: :findBaseDir
 92: IF EXIST "%WDIR%"\.mvn goto baseDirFound
 93: cd ..
 94: IF "%WDIR%"=="%CD%" goto baseDirNotFound
 95: set WDIR=%CD%
 96: goto findBaseDir
 97: 
 98: :baseDirFound
 99: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%WDIR%
100: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
101: goto endDetectBaseDir
102: 
103: :baseDirNotFound
104: set MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR=%EXEC_DIR%
105: cd "%EXEC_DIR%"
106: 
107: :endDetectBaseDir
108: 
109: IF NOT EXIST "%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config" goto endReadAdditionalConfig
110: 
111: @setlocal EnableExtensions EnableDelayedExpansion
112: for /F "usebackq delims=" %%a in ("%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\jvm.config") do set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=!JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS! %%a
113: @endlocal & set JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS=%JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS%
114: 
115: :endReadAdditionalConfig
116: 
117: SET MAVEN_JAVA_EXE="%JAVA_HOME%\bin\java.exe"
118: 
119: set WRAPPER_JAR="%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%\.mvn\wrapper\maven-wrapper.jar"
120: set WRAPPER_LAUNCHER=org.apache.maven.wrapper.MavenWrapperMain
121: 
122: %MAVEN_JAVA_EXE% %JVM_CONFIG_MAVEN_PROPS% %MAVEN_OPTS% %MAVEN_DEBUG_OPTS% -classpath %WRAPPER_JAR% "-Dmaven.multiModuleProjectDirectory=%MAVEN_PROJECTBASEDIR%" %WRAPPER_LAUNCHER% %MAVEN_CONFIG% %*
123: if ERRORLEVEL 1 goto error
124: goto end
125: 
126: :error
127: set ERROR_CODE=1
128: 
129: :end
130: @endlocal & set ERROR_CODE=%ERROR_CODE%
131: 
132: if not "%MAVEN_SKIP_RC%" == "" goto skipRcPost
133: @REM check for post script, once with legacy .bat ending and once with .cmd ending
134: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.bat"
135: if exist "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd" call "%HOME%\mavenrc_post.cmd"
136: :skipRcPost
137: 
138: @REM pause the script if MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE is set to 'on'
139: if "%MAVEN_BATCH_PAUSE%" == "on" pause
140: 
141: if "%MAVEN_TERMINATE_CMD%" == "on" exit %ERROR_CODE%
142: 
143: exit /B %ERROR_CODE%

File: wait-for-it.sh
================
  1: cmdname=$(basename $0)
  2: echoerr() { if [[ $QUIET -ne 1 ]]; then echo "$@" 1>&2; fi }
  3: usage()
  4: {
  5:     cat << USAGE >&2
  6: Usage:
  7:     $cmdname host:port [-s] [-t timeout] [-- command args]
  8:     -h HOST | --host=HOST       Host or IP under test
  9:     -p PORT | --port=PORT       TCP port under test
 10:                                 Alternatively, you specify the host and port as host:port
 11:     -s | --strict               Only execute subcommand if the test succeeds
 12:     -q | --quiet                Don't output any status messages
 13:     -t TIMEOUT | --timeout=TIMEOUT
 14:                                 Timeout in seconds, zero for no timeout
 15:     -- COMMAND ARGS             Execute command with args after the test finishes
 16: USAGE
 17:     exit 1
 18: }
 19: wait_for()
 20: {
 21:     if [[ $TIMEOUT -gt 0 ]]; then
 22:         echoerr "$cmdname: waiting $TIMEOUT seconds for $HOST:$PORT"
 23:     else
 24:         echoerr "$cmdname: waiting for $HOST:$PORT without a timeout"
 25:     fi
 26:     start_ts=$(date +%s)
 27:     while :
 28:     do
 29:         if [[ $ISBUSY -eq 1 ]]; then
 30:             nc -z $HOST $PORT
 31:             result=$?
 32:         else
 33:             (echo > /dev/tcp/$HOST/$PORT) >/dev/null 2>&1
 34:             result=$?
 35:         fi
 36:         if [[ $result -eq 0 ]]; then
 37:             end_ts=$(date +%s)
 38:             echoerr "$cmdname: $HOST:$PORT is available after $((end_ts - start_ts)) seconds"
 39:             break
 40:         fi
 41:         sleep 1
 42:     done
 43:     return $result
 44: }
 45: wait_for_wrapper()
 46: {
 47:     if [[ $QUIET -eq 1 ]]; then
 48:         timeout $BUSYTIMEFLAG $TIMEOUT $0 --quiet --child --host=$HOST --port=$PORT --timeout=$TIMEOUT &
 49:     else
 50:         timeout $BUSYTIMEFLAG $TIMEOUT $0 --child --host=$HOST --port=$PORT --timeout=$TIMEOUT &
 51:     fi
 52:     PID=$!
 53:     trap "kill -INT -$PID" INT
 54:     wait $PID
 55:     RESULT=$?
 56:     if [[ $RESULT -ne 0 ]]; then
 57:         echoerr "$cmdname: timeout occurred after waiting $TIMEOUT seconds for $HOST:$PORT"
 58:     fi
 59:     return $RESULT
 60: }
 61: while [[ $
 62: do
 63:     case "$1" in
 64:         *:* )
 65:         hostport=(${1//:/ })
 66:         HOST=${hostport[0]}
 67:         PORT=${hostport[1]}
 68:         shift 1
 69:         ;;
 70:         --child)
 71:         CHILD=1
 72:         shift 1
 73:         ;;
 74:         -q | --quiet)
 75:         QUIET=1
 76:         shift 1
 77:         ;;
 78:         -s | --strict)
 79:         STRICT=1
 80:         shift 1
 81:         ;;
 82:         -h)
 83:         HOST="$2"
 84:         if [[ $HOST == "" ]]; then break; fi
 85:         shift 2
 86:         ;;
 87:         --host=*)
 88:         HOST="${1
 89:         shift 1
 90:         ;;
 91:         -p)
 92:         PORT="$2"
 93:         if [[ $PORT == "" ]]; then break; fi
 94:         shift 2
 95:         ;;
 96:         --port=*)
 97:         PORT="${1
 98:         shift 1
 99:         ;;
100:         -t)
101:         TIMEOUT="$2"
102:         if [[ $TIMEOUT == "" ]]; then break; fi
103:         shift 2
104:         ;;
105:         --timeout=*)
106:         TIMEOUT="${1
107:         shift 1
108:         ;;
109:         --)
110:         shift
111:         CLI=("$@")
112:         break
113:         ;;
114:         --help)
115:         usage
116:         ;;
117:         *)
118:         echoerr "Unknown argument: $1"
119:         usage
120:         ;;
121:     esac
122: done
123: if [[ "$HOST" == "" || "$PORT" == "" ]]; then
124:     echoerr "Error: you need to provide a host and port to test."
125:     usage
126: fi
127: TIMEOUT=${TIMEOUT:-15}
128: STRICT=${STRICT:-0}
129: CHILD=${CHILD:-0}
130: QUIET=${QUIET:-0}
131: # check to see if timeout is from busybox?
132: # check to see if timeout is from busybox?
133: TIMEOUT_PATH=$(realpath $(which timeout))
134: if [[ $TIMEOUT_PATH =~ "busybox" ]]; then
135:         ISBUSY=1
136:         BUSYTIMEFLAG="-t"
137: else
138:         ISBUSY=0
139:         BUSYTIMEFLAG=""
140: fi
141: if [[ $CHILD -gt 0 ]]; then
142:     wait_for
143:     RESULT=$?
144:     exit $RESULT
145: else
146:     if [[ $TIMEOUT -gt 0 ]]; then
147:         wait_for_wrapper
148:         RESULT=$?
149:     else
150:         wait_for
151:         RESULT=$?
152:     fi
153: fi
154: if [[ $CLI != "" ]]; then
155:     if [[ $RESULT -ne 0 && $STRICT -eq 1 ]]; then
156:         echoerr "$cmdname: strict mode, refusing to execute subprocess"
157:         exit $RESULT
158:     fi
159:     exec "${CLI[@]}"
160: else
161:     exit $RESULT
162: fi
